# Neural architecture simulator screen

This is a short learning screen, not evidence of convergence or statistical superiority. Independent training seeds, not evaluation worlds, determine replication. Three seeds remain weak evidence. Means and paired changes below are descriptive; no significance claims are made.

All **105 main cases plus 14 exploratory learning-rate cases** are complete and passed the [structural/checkpoint audit](../evaluations/architecture-study-20261020/v5-final-119-audit.json). Read the [reviewed findings](NN_ARCHITECTURE_FINDINGS.md) for interpretation, fresh simulator replay checks, 15 map diagnostics, and known test/trajectory limitations. This generated metric report alone does not establish behavioral validity.

Source root: `/mnt/zfs-storage/All Drives/Brian Data/Projects/AutoDrive/evaluations/architecture-study-20261020/matrix`. Completed cases: **119**; excluded/incomplete metadata: **0**.

See [research and evaluation rationale](NN_ARCHITECTURE_RESEARCH.md). Policy inputs use simulated perception; truth may be used to calculate evaluation outcomes. This summary reads recorded metrics, not replay frames, and does not verify dashboard freshness.

`entity_attention` is the tested entity Transformer: two four-head self-attention/feed-forward/residual encoder blocks over masked perception entities. Registry names in the tables remain unchanged.

Acquisition is the recorded cumulative FUEL acquisition count, which can count reacquisition. Score difference is blue minus red. Δ columns subtract each seed's initialized evaluation before averaging. Evaluation uses greedy masked actions; training samples categorical actions. Training seconds exclude evaluation. GPU slots 0 and 1 are the same AMD WX 9100 model; device slot is ignored for sample-learning grouping and preserved per case. Wall times are descriptive and can reflect hardware placement or overlapping jobs; they are not isolated speed benchmarks. Other protocol differences remain separate.

The experimental offense_vs_deterministic_defense scenario places blue neural offense against a deterministic red defender. Its research factory uses generic strategic execution and lacks deterministic-only intake/scoring refinements; results describe this limited execution path, not a production offense replacement.

On HIP, disable_miopen defaults true to avoid the residual CNN wrw_reduction_hip weight-gradient fault. The same ATen convolution backend is used across alternatives. Backend qualification: [neural-backend-disabled.json](</mnt/zfs-storage/All Drives/Brian Data/Projects/AutoDrive/evaluations/architecture-study-20261020/qualification/neural-backend-disabled.json>). That record covers three field encoders at batches 32 and 96; all seven learning-matrix alternatives use the selected backend. Qualification and integration artifacts are excluded from the learning matrix.

Pickup scenarios mask scoring, interception, and ferry actions 4/5/6. This prevents the previously observed collect/release/reacquire loop. Earlier unrestricted-pickup trials were moved to outdated-unrestricted-pickup and marked OUTDATED.json; they are excluded from this matrix. No architecture conclusion follows from those trials.

## Learning curves

Thin translucent lines are individual training seeds; thick lines are means at checkpoints shared by all available seeds for that model. Curves remain separate by learning rate and protocol. Partial seed availability is not a balanced comparison. Transitions count learned-agent decisions; 3v3 has three learned agents per world. Compare physical time using metadata and world_physics_ticks.

## Reproduction and settings

Reproduce this report:

```bash
/usr/bin/python3 scripts/summarize_architecture_study.py --root '/mnt/zfs-storage/All Drives/Brian Data/Projects/AutoDrive/evaluations/architecture-study-20261020/matrix' --output '/mnt/zfs-storage/All Drives/Brian Data/Projects/AutoDrive/docs/NN_ARCHITECTURE_RESULTS.md'
```

Case metadata preserves the exact full CLI configuration, reward expression, seed, model size, and source hashes. The following are protocol settings excluding matrix selectors, output location, and device slot. Fixed PPO implementation details remain in the source identified by each case's provenance.

### P1

```json
{
  "action_repeat": 10,
  "disable_miopen": true,
  "envs": 8,
  "epochs": 2,
  "eval_envs": 4,
  "eval_every": 2,
  "eval_ticks": 200,
  "gamma": 0.993,
  "horizon": 200,
  "minibatch_size": 32,
  "rollout_steps": 20,
  "updates": 4
}
```

```bash
.venv/bin/python -m frc_defense.architecture_benchmark --architectures baseline_mlp --scenarios pickup_scattered --seeds 19 --learning-rates 0.0003 --output '/mnt/zfs-storage/All Drives/Brian Data/Projects/AutoDrive/evaluations/architecture-study-20261020/matrix' --device cuda:1 --action-repeat 10 --disable-miopen --envs 8 --epochs 2 --eval-envs 4 --eval-every 2 --eval-ticks 200 --gamma 0.993 --horizon 200 --minibatch-size 32 --rollout-steps 20 --updates 4
```
